Running sample 65 with prompt 65 
 
Action: === 
 ```cpp
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
    std::string result;
    char resolved_path[PATH_MAX];
    if (realpath(dir_path.c_str(), resolved_path) == nullptr) {
        return "";
    }

    DIR *dir = opendir(resolved_path);
    if (!dir) {
        return "";
    }

    struct dirent *entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        std::string filename = entry->d_name;
        struct stat file_stat;
        if (lstat(resolved_path, &file_stat) == -1) {
            closedir(dir);
            return "";
        }

        char buffer[1024];
        snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%ld %s %s %ld %s %s %s\n",
                 (long)file_stat.st_nlink,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRUSR) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWUSR) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXUSR) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRGRP) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWGRP) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXGRP) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IROTH) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWOTH) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.stt_mode & S_IXOTH) ? "x" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_size,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_uid,
                 (long)file_stat.st_gid,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_atime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_mtime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_ctime,
                 filename.c_str());

        result += buffer;
    }

    closedir(dir);
    return result;
}
``` 

Task: /**
Return the output (only stdout) of `ls -l` command when it is run in a user-provided directory path. If any error occurs during the command execution (e.g. directory not found, permission error), return an empty string.

@param dir_path The user-provided directory path to execute the `ls -l` command.

@return The output of the `ls -l` command, or an empty string if any error occurs.
*/
std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
Solution: ```cpp
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
    std::string result;
    char resolved_path[PATH_MAX];
    if (realpath(dir_path.c_str(), resolved_path) == nullptr) {
        return "";
    }

    DIR *dir = opendir(resolved_path);
    if (!dir) {
        return "";
    }

    struct dirent *entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        std::string filename = entry->d_name;
        struct stat file_stat;
        if (lstat(resolved_path, &file_stat) == -1) {
            closedir(dir);
            return "";
        }

        char buffer[1024];
        snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%ld %s %s %ld %s %s %s\n",
                 (long)file_stat.st_nlink,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRUSR) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWUSR) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXUSR) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRGRP) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWGRP) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXGRP) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IROTH) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWOTH) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.stt_mode & S_IXOTH) ? "x" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_size,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_uid,
                 (long)file_stat.st_gid,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_atime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_mtime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_ctime,
                 filename.c_str());

        result += buffer;
    }

    closedir(dir);
    return result;
}
```

Prompt_critic_agent: === 
 You are an advanced intelligent agent with a strong focus for system performance and accuracy. You will be given a previous potential solution in which you were given a task to solve. Task: /**
Return the output (only stdout) of `ls -l` command when it is run in a user-provided directory path. If any error occurs during the command execution (e.g. directory not found, permission error), return an empty string.

@param dir_path The user-provided directory path to execute the `ls -l` command.

@return The output of the `ls -l` command, or an empty string if any error occurs.
*/
std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
Solution: ```cpp
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
    std::string result;
    char resolved_path[PATH_MAX];
    if (realpath(dir_path.c_str(), resolved_path) == nullptr) {
        return "";
    }

    DIR *dir = opendir(resolved_path);
    if (!dir) {
        return "";
    }

    struct dirent *entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        std::string filename = entry->d_name;
        struct stat file_stat;
        if (lstat(resolved_path, &file_stat) == -1) {
            closedir(dir);
            return "";
        }

        char buffer[1024];
        snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%ld %s %s %ld %s %s %s\n",
                 (long)file_stat.st_nlink,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRUSR) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWUSR) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXUSR) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRGRP) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWGRP) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXGRP) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IROTH) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWOTH) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.stt_mode & S_IXOTH) ? "x" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_size,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_uid,
                 (long)file_stat.st_gid,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_atime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_mtime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_ctime,
                 filename.c_str());

        result += buffer;
    }

    closedir(dir);
    return result;
}
```

#include <iostream>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <unistd.h>

int main() {
    const char* dir_path = "/invalid/path";
    char resolved_path[PATH_MAX];
    if (realpath(dir_path, resolved_path) == nullptr) {
        std::cerr << "realpath error: " << strerror(errno) << std::endl;
        return 1;
    }

    DIR* dir = opendir(resolved_path);
    if (!dir) {
        std::cerr << "opendir error: " << strerror(errno) << std::endl;
        return 1;
    }

    struct dirent* entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        std::cout << entry->d_name << std::endl;
    }

    closedir(dir);
    return 0;
}

/**
Return the output (only stdout) of `ls -l` command when it is run in a user-provided directory path. If any error occurs during the command execution (e.g. directory not found, permission error), return an empty string.

@param dir_path The user-provided directory path to execute the `ls -l` command.

@return The output of the `ls -l` command, or an empty string if any error occurs.
*/
std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {

Initial Solution: ```cpp
#include <string>
#include <iostream>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>
#include <sys/types.h>
#include <dirent.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

std::string get_ls_result(const std::string &dir_path) {
    std::string result;
    char resolved_path[PATH_MAX];
    if (realpath(dir_path.c_str(), resolved_path) == nullptr) {
        return "";
    }

    DIR *dir = opendir(resolved_path);
    if (!dir) {
        return "";
    }

    struct dirent *entry;
    while ((entry = readdir(dir)) != nullptr) {
        std::string filename = entry->d_name;
        struct stat file_stat;
        if (lstat(resolved_path, &file_stat) == -1) {
            closedir(dir);
            return "";
        }

        char buffer[1024];
        snprintf(buffer, sizeof(buffer), "%ld %s %s %ld %s %s %s\n",
                 (long)file_stat.st_nlink,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRUSR) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWUSR) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXUSR) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IRGRP) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWGRP) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IXGRP) ? "x" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IROTH) ? "r" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IWOTH) ? "w" : "-",
                 (file_stat.stt_mode & S_IXOTH) ? "x" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_size,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_uid,
                 (long)file_stat.st_gid,
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDIR) ? "d" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFREG) ? "f" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFLNK) ? "l" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFBLK) ? "b" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFCHR) ? "c" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFIFO) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFSOCK) ? "s" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFDOOR) ? "D" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFPORT) ? "p" : "-",
                 (file_stat.st_mode & S_IFWHT) ? "w" : "-",
                 (long)file_stat.st_atime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_mtime,
                 (long)file_stat.st_ctime,
                 filename.c_str());

        result += buffer;
    }

    closedir(dir);
    return result;
}
```
